\[
  \mathcal F_{\mathbb A,n}
  :=
  \Bigl\{
  (\theta,\rho,x)\mapsto
  \chi^{\mathbb A}_{m,\ell}(\theta,\rho)\,
  (-1)^{\langle \xi,x\rangle}
  :
  (m,\ell,\xi)\in\mathbb Z^{2}\times I_n
  \Bigr\}
\]
\[
  \widehat\mu_{T}^{(\mathbb A,n)}(m,\ell,\xi)
  :=
  \frac1T
  \sum_{t=0}^{T-1}
  \chi^{\mathbb A}_{m,\ell}(\theta_t,\rho_t)\,
  (-1)^{\langle \xi,x_t\rangle}
\]
\[
  \mathsf{Bias}_{T}^{(\mathbb A,n)}
  :=
  \sup_{(m,\ell,\xi)\neq(0,0,0)}
  \left|
  \widehat\mu_{T}^{(\mathbb A,n)}(m,\ell,\xi)
  \right|
\]
\[
  \mathsf{Disc}_{T,\mathcal F_{\mathbb A,n}}
  :=
  \sup_{f\in\mathcal F_{\mathbb A,n}}
  \left|
  \frac1T\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}f(\theta_t,\rho_t,x_t)
  -
  \int f\,d\mu_{\mathbb A,n}
  \right|
\]
\[
  \mathsf{PR}_{\mathbb A,n}(T,\varepsilon)
  \Longleftrightarrow
  \mathsf{Bias}_{T}^{(\mathbb A,n)}\le\varepsilon
\]
\[
  \mathsf{PR}_{\mathbb A,n}(T,\varepsilon)
  \Longleftrightarrow
  \mathsf{Disc}_{T,\mathcal F_{\mathbb A,n}}\le\varepsilon
\]

  \mathsf{Mix}_{\mathbb A,n}(T)

  \mu_{T}^{(\mathbb A,n)}
  :=
  \frac1T\sum_{t=0}^{T-1}\delta_{(\theta_t,\rho_t,x_t)}

\[
  U_{n}
  :=
  2^{-n}\sum_{x\in I_n}\delta_{x}

\[
  \mathcal D_{s,d}^{\mathrm{Walsh}}
  :=
  \left\{
  D(x)
  =
  \sum_{\substack{\xi\in I_n\\|\xi|\le d}}
  \alpha_{\xi}(-1)^{\langle \xi,x\rangle}
  :
  \sum_{\xi}|\alpha_{\xi}|\le s
  \right\}
\]
\[
  \mathsf{Adv}_{T}^{\mathrm{Walsh}}(\mathbb A,n;s,d)
  :=
  \sup_{D\in\mathcal D_{s,d}^{\mathrm{Walsh}}}
  \left|
  \mathbb E_{\mu_{T}^{(\mathbb A,n)}}[D]
  -
  \mathbb E_{U_n}[D]
  \right|
\]
\[
  \mathsf{Pred}_{k}^{(\mathbb A,n)}
  :=
  \sup_{P:\{0,1\}^{k}\to\{0,1\}}
  \left|
  \Pr\bigl[P(x_0,\dots,x_{k-1})=x_k\bigr]
  -
  \frac12
  \right|
\]
\[
  \mathsf{Corr}_{T,\mathbf h}^{(\mathbb A,n)}
  :=
  \left|
  \frac1T
  \sum_{t=0}^{T-1}

  x_t+x_{t+h_1}+\cdots+x_{t+h_r}
  }
  \right|

%% ── Orthodox quantum measurement: density operators, observables, POVM, Born rule ──
%%
\[
  \mathcal D_{\mathbb A,n}
  :=
  \Bigl\{
  \varrho\in\mathrm{End}(X_{\mathbb A,n}^{\mathrm{hyb}}):
  \varrho\ge0,\ 
  \operatorname{Tr}(\varrho)=1
  \Bigr\}
\]
\[
  \mathsf{Obs}_{\mathbb A,n}
  :=
  \Bigl\{
  A\in\mathrm{End}(X_{\mathbb A,n}^{\mathrm{hyb}}):
  A=A^{*}
  \Bigr\}
\]
\[
  A=\sum_{j}\lambda_j\Pi^{A}_{j},
  \qquad
  (\Pi^{A}_{j})^{2}=\Pi^{A}_{j}=(\Pi^{A}_{j})^{*},
  \qquad
  \sum_{j}\Pi^{A}_{j}=I
\]
\[
  p_{A}(j\mid\varrho):=\operatorname{Tr}(\Pi^{A}_{j}\varrho)
\]
\[
  \varrho\longmapsto
  \varrho_{A,j}
  :=
  \frac{\Pi^{A}_{j}\varrho\Pi^{A}_{j}}{p_{A}(j\mid\varrho)}
\]
\[
  \mathsf{POVM}_{\mathbb A,n}
  :=
  \Bigl\{
  \{M_{\alpha}\}_{\alpha}:
  \sum_{\alpha}M_{\alpha}^{*}M_{\alpha}=I
  \Bigr\}
\]
\[
  E_{\alpha}:=M_{\alpha}^{*}M_{\alpha},
  \qquad
  p(\alpha\mid\varrho):=\operatorname{Tr}(E_{\alpha}\varrho)
\]
\[
  \varrho\longmapsto
  \varrho_{\alpha}
  :=
  \frac{M_{\alpha}\varrho M_{\alpha}^{*}}{p(\alpha\mid\varrho)}
\]
\[
  \mathcal P_{x}^{\mathrm{hyb}}:=\iota_x\pi_x,
  \qquad
  (\mathcal P_{x}^{\mathrm{hyb}})^{2}=\mathcal P_{x}^{\mathrm{hyb}},
  \qquad
  \sum_{x\in I_n}\mathcal P_{x}^{\mathrm{hyb}}=I
\]
\[
  p(x\mid\varrho):=\operatorname{Tr}(\mathcal P_{x}^{\mathrm{hyb}}\varrho)
\]
\[
  \varrho\longmapsto
  \varrho_{x}
  :=
  \frac{\mathcal P_{x}^{\mathrm{hyb}}\varrho\mathcal P_{x}^{\mathrm{hyb}}}{p(x\mid\varrho)}
\]
\[
  [\mathcal P_{x}^{\mathrm{hyb}},\Pi_{j}^{A}]=0
  \Longrightarrow
  p(x,j\mid\varrho)
  :=
  \operatorname{Tr}(\mathcal P_{x}^{\mathrm{hyb}}\Pi_{j}^{A}\varrho)
\]
\[
  \sum_{x,j}p(x,j\mid\varrho)=1
\]
\[
  \mathsf{PVM}_{\mathbb A,n}
  :=
  \Bigl\{
  \{\Pi_j\}_j:
  \Pi_j^2=\Pi_j=\Pi_j^{*},
  \ \Pi_j\Pi_k=\delta_{j,k}\Pi_j,
  \ \sum_j \Pi_j=I
  \Bigr\}
\]
\[
  \mathsf{CPTP}_{\mathbb A,n}
  :=
  \Bigl\{
  \Phi:
  \End(X_{\mathbb A,n}^{\mathrm{hyb}})
  	o
  \End(X_{\mathbb A,n}^{\mathrm{hyb}})
  :
  \exists\{K_r\}_{r}
  \ \Phi(\varrho)=\sum_{r}K_r\varrho K_r^{*},\
  \sum_{r}K_r^{*}K_r=I
  \Bigr\}
\]
\[
  \mathsf{Instr}_{\mathbb A,n}
  :=
  \Bigl\{
  \{\Phi_{\alpha}\}_{\alpha}:
  \forall\alpha\ \Phi_{\alpha}\in\mathrm{CP},\
  \sum_{\alpha}\Phi_{\alpha}\in\mathsf{CPTP}_{\mathbb A,n}
  \Bigr\}
\]
\[
  p_{\Phi}(\alpha\mid\varrho)
  :=
  \operatorname{Tr}\!\bigl(\Phi_{\alpha}(\varrho)\bigr)
\]
\[
  \varrho\longmapsto
  \varrho_{\Phi,\alpha}
  :=
  \frac{\Phi_{\alpha}(\varrho)}
  {p_{\Phi}(\alpha\mid\varrho)}
\]
\[
  \mathbb P_{\varrho}^{\mathsf E}(B)
  :=
  \operatorname{Tr}\!\bigl(\varrho\,\mathsf E(B)\bigr),
  \qquad
  \mathbb P_{\varrho}^{\mathsf F}(B)
  :=
  \operatorname{Tr}\!\bigl(\varrho\,\mathsf F(B)\bigr)
\]
\[
  \varrho_{B}^{\mathsf E}
  :=
  \frac{\mathsf E(B)\varrho\,\mathsf E(B)}
       {\operatorname{Tr}(\varrho\,\mathsf E(B))}
  \qquad
  \bigl(
  \operatorname{Tr}(\varrho\,\mathsf E(B))\neq 0
  \bigr)
\]
\[
  \varrho_{B}^{\mathsf F}
  :=
  \frac{\sqrt{\mathsf F(B)}\,\varrho\,\sqrt{\mathsf F(B)}}
       {\operatorname{Tr}(\varrho\,\mathsf F(B))}
  \qquad
  \bigl(
  \operatorname{Tr}(\varrho\,\mathsf F(B))\neq 0
  \bigr)
\]
\[
  \mathsf{Naimark}_{\mathbb A,n}

  \mathcal K_{\mathbb A,n},\,
  V_{\mathbb A,n},\,

  \Bigr)
\]
\[
  E_\alpha=V_{\mathbb A,n}^{*}P_\alpha V_{\mathbb A,n}
\]
\[
  \Delta_{\varrho}(A)^2
  :=
  \operatorname{Tr}(\varrho A^2)-\operatorname{Tr}(\varrho A)^2
\]
\[
  \operatorname{Cov}_{\varrho}(A,B)
  :=
  \operatorname{Tr}(\varrho AB)-\operatorname{Tr}(\varrho A)\operatorname{Tr}(\varrho B)
\]
\[
  \Delta_{\varrho}(A)^2\Delta_{\varrho}(B)^2
  \ge
  \frac14\left|
  \operatorname{Tr}\bigl(\varrho[A,B]\bigr)
  \right|^2

%% ── Hybrid algebra A_hyb universality theorem ──
%%
\[
  \mathcal A_{\mathbb A,n}^{\mathrm{hyb}}
  :=

  \forall T\in\mathcal A_{\mathbb A,n}^{\mathrm{hyb}}\ \exists C\in\langle\mathcal G_{\mathbb A,n}^{\mathrm{HES}}\rangle:\ C=T

\[
  \Omega_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(\mathbb A,n)}
  \bigl((g;u,v),(h;u',v')\bigr)
  :=
  \Omega_{\mathbb A}(g,h)+\Omega_{\mathrm{bool}}(v,u')
\]
\[
  \delta\Omega_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(\mathbb A,n)}
  =
  \delta\Omega_{\mathbb A}
  +
  \delta\Omega_{\mathrm{bool}}
  =
  0

\[
  \widetilde G_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(\mathbb A,n)}
  :=
  \bigl((I_n\times G_{\mathbb A}\times I_n)\times Z,\cdot\bigr)
\]
\[
  ((u,g,v),a)\cdot((u',h,v'),b)
  :=
  \Bigl(
  (u\oplus u',gh,v\oplus v'),\,
  a+b+\Omega_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(\mathbb A,n)}((g;u,v),(h;u',v'))
  \Bigr)
\]
\[
\begin{aligned}
  &\Bigl(((u,g,v),a)\cdot((u',h,v'),b)\Bigr)\cdot((u'',k,v''),c)\\
  &=
  ((u,g,v),a)\cdot\Bigl(((u',h,v'),b)\cdot((u'',k,v''),c)\Bigr)
\end{aligned}
\]
\[
  F_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(\mathbb A,n)}
  \bigl((g;u,v),(h;u',v')\bigr)
  :=
  F_{\mathbb A}(g,h)+\Omega_{\mathrm{bool}}(v,u')

  \mathrm{UFE}_{\mathrm{Mir},\mathbb A,n}^{\mathrm{hyb}}
  :=
  F_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(\mathbb A,n)}-\Omega_{\mathrm{tot}}^{(\mathbb A,n)}=0

  \mathsf{Univ}_{\mathbb A,n}

%% ── VI: Pseudorandomness via small-bias/indistinguishability ──

  G_{\mathbb A,n,N}^{\Mir}\ \text{is }(t,\varepsilon)\text{-pseudorandom}

  &=\exp\!\left(-i\frac{2\pi m}{\ln\phi}(\rho+t\ln\phi)\right)\,

  &=\exp\!\left(-i\frac{2\pi m}{\ln\phi}\rho-i\frac{2\pi m}{\ln\phi}t\ln\phi\right)\,
    \Psi\!\left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{2}t,\ \rho+t\ln\phi\right)\\
  &=\exp\!\left(-i\frac{2\pi m}{\ln\phi}\rho-i2\pi mt\right)\,
    \Psi\!\left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{2}t,\ \rho+t\ln\phi\right)\\
  &=e^{-i2\pi mt}\,
    \exp\!\left(-i\frac{2\pi m}{\ln\phi}\rho\right)\,
    \Psi\!\left(\theta-\frac{\pi}{2}t,\ \rho+t\ln\phi\right)\\
  &=e^{-i2\pi mt}\,(\MM_m\UU_t\Psi)(\theta,\rho)
\end{aligned}
\]

  \UU_t\MM_m=e^{-i2\pi mt}\,\MM_m\UU_t

\UU\Pi_N-\Pi_N
&=
\frac{1}{2N+1}\left(\sum_{n=-N}^{N}\UU^{n+1}-\sum_{n=-N}^{N}\UU^n\right)\\
&=
\frac{\UU^{N+1}-\UU^{-N}}{2N+1}
\end{aligned}

\end{aligned}

J_w^{k}(f):=\sum_{n=0}^{k}\frac{f^{(n)}(w)}{n!}\varepsilon^n,\qquad
f(w+\varepsilon)=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{f^{(n)}(w)}{n!}\varepsilon^n,\qquad
\varepsilon^{k+1}=0

\[
\begin{aligned}
  f(w+\varepsilon)
  &=\sum_{n=0}^{k}\frac{f^{(n)}(w)}{n!}\varepsilon^n
   +\sum_{n=k+1}^{\infty}\frac{f^{(n)}(w)}{n!}\varepsilon^{k+1}\varepsilon^{n-k-1}\\
  &\equiv\sum_{n=0}^{k}\frac{f^{(n)}(w)}{n!}\varepsilon^n
  =J_w^{k}(f)\pmod{\varepsilon^{k+1}}
\end{aligned}
\]
\[
J_w^{k}(fg)
=\sum_{n=0}^{k}\sum_{j=0}^{n}\frac{f^{(j)}(w)\,g^{(n-j)}(w)}{j!(n-j)!}\varepsilon^n
\equiv
\left(\sum_{j=0}^{k}\frac{f^{(j)}(w)}{j!}\varepsilon^j\right)
\left(\sum_{m=0}^{k}\frac{g^{(m)}(w)}{m!}\varepsilon^m\right)
\pmod{\varepsilon^{k+1}}
\]
\[
J_w^{k}(fg)\equiv J_w^{k}(f)\,J_w^{k}(g)\pmod{\varepsilon^{k+1}}

  u^{-1}=1-X+\tfrac{1}{2}X^{2}\pmod{\widehat I_{d}^{\,3}},
  \qquad

\[
  S:=X+Y,
  \qquad
  P:=\tfrac{1}{2}X^{2}+XY+\tfrac{1}{2}Y^{2}
\]
\[
  u^{-1}v^{-1}=1-S+P,
  \qquad
  uv=1+S+P
  \pmod{\widehat I_{d}^{\,3}}
\]
\[
  [u,v]
  =u^{-1}v^{-1}uv
  =(1-S+P)(1+S+P)
  =1+2P-S^{2}
  \pmod{\widehat I_{d}^{\,3}}
\]
\[
  S^{2}=(X+Y)^{2}=X^{2}+XY+YX+Y^{2},\qquad
  2P-S^{2}=XY-YX=[X,Y]
\]
\[
  [u,v]=1+[X,Y]\pmod{\widehat I_{d}^{\,3}}

\begin{aligned}
  (\theta,\rho(\theta))\mapsto\widehat U_{\phi}(\theta,\rho(\theta))
  &=\left(\theta+\frac{\pi}{2},\ \rho(\theta)-\ln\phi\right),\\
  \widehat U_{\phi}\text{-invariance:}\quad
  \left(\theta+\frac{\pi}{2},\ \rho\!\left(\theta+\frac{\pi}{2}\right)\right)
  &=\left(\theta+\frac{\pi}{2},\ \rho(\theta)-\ln\phi\right),\\
  \rho\!\left(\theta+\frac{\pi}{2}\right)&=\rho(\theta)-\ln\phi,\\
  k&:=\frac{2\ln\phi}{\pi},\\
  p(\theta)&:=\rho(\theta)+k\theta,\\
  p\!\left(\theta+\frac\pi2\right)
  &=\rho\!\left(\theta+\frac\pi2\right)+k\left(\theta+\frac\pi2\right)\\
  &=(\rho(\theta)-\ln\phi)+k\theta+k\frac\pi2\\
  &=(\rho(\theta)-\ln\phi)+k\theta+\left(\frac{2\ln\phi}{\pi}\right)\frac{\pi}{2}\\
  &=(\rho(\theta)-\ln\phi)+k\theta+\ln\phi\\
  &=\rho(\theta)+k\theta\\
  &=p(\theta),\\
  \rho(\theta)&=-k\theta+p(\theta),\\
  r(\theta)
  &:=e^{\rho(\theta)}
   =e^{-k\theta+p(\theta)}
   =e^{p(\theta)}e^{-k\theta},\\
  p(\theta)\equiv B&\Rightarrow
  \rho(\theta)=B-k\theta,\\
  p(\theta)\equiv B&\Rightarrow
  r(\theta)=e^{B}e^{-k\theta}=r_0e^{-k\theta},\qquad r_0:=e^B,\\
  k=\frac{2\ln\phi}{\pi}&\Rightarrow r(\theta)=r_0e^{-(2\ln\phi/\pi)\theta}
  =r_0\phi^{-2\theta/\pi}
\end{aligned}

\begin{aligned}
  r(\theta)&=r_{0}\phi^{-2\theta/\pi}=r_{0}e^{-(2\ln\phi/\pi)\theta}, \\
  \frac{dr}{d\theta}
  &=r_{0}\frac{d}{d\theta}\left(e^{-(2\ln\phi/\pi)\theta}\right)
  =r_{0}\left(-\frac{2\ln\phi}{\pi}\right)e^{-(2\ln\phi/\pi)\theta}
  =-\frac{2\ln\phi}{\pi}\,r(\theta), \\
  x(\theta)&:=r(\theta)\cos\theta,\qquad y(\theta):=r(\theta)\sin\theta, \\
  dx&=d(r\cos\theta)=dr\cos\theta+r\,d(\cos\theta)=dr\cos\theta-r\sin\theta\,d\theta, \\
  dy&=d(r\sin\theta)=dr\sin\theta+r\,d(\sin\theta)=dr\sin\theta+r\cos\theta\,d\theta, \\
  ds^{2}
  &:=dx^{2}+dy^{2} \\
  &=dr^{2}(\cos^{2}\theta+\sin^{2}\theta)
    +r^{2}d\theta^{2}(\sin^{2}\theta+\cos^{2}\theta) \\
  &\quad +2dr\,r\,d\theta\bigl(-\sin\theta\cos\theta+\sin\theta\cos\theta\bigr) \\
  &=dr^{2}+r^{2}d\theta^{2}
  =dr^{2}+(r\,d\theta)^{2}, \\
  ds^{2}
  &=\left(\frac{dr}{d\theta}\right)^{2}d\theta^{2}+r^{2}d\theta^{2}
  =\left[\left(\frac{dr}{d\theta}\right)^{2}+r^{2}\right]d\theta^{2} \\
  &=\left[\left(\frac{2\ln\phi}{\pi}\right)^{2}r^{2}+r^{2}\right]d\theta^{2}
  =r^{2}\left(1+\left(\frac{2\ln\phi}{\pi}\right)^{2}\right)d\theta^{2}, \\

\end{aligned}

  &=d\rho\,d\rho-i\,d\rho\,d\theta+i\,d\theta\,d\rho-i^{2}\,d\theta\,d\theta\\

  \det(T)&=\left(\frac{1}{\phi}\right)^{2}\det(J)=\frac{1}{\phi^{2}}, \\

\begin{aligned}
  J&=\begin{pmatrix}0&-1\\[2pt]1&0\end{pmatrix},\qquad
  J^{\mathsf T}=\begin{pmatrix}0&1\\[2pt]-1&0\end{pmatrix}=-J, \\
  JJ^{\mathsf T}
  &=\begin{pmatrix}0&-1\\[2pt]1&0\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}0&1\\[2pt]-1&0\end{pmatrix}
   =\begin{pmatrix}1&0\\[2pt]0&1\end{pmatrix}=I_{2}, \\
  &\Rightarrow\ J^{-1}=J^{\mathsf T}, \\
  T&=\frac{1}{\phi}J, \\
  T^{-1}
  &=\left(\frac{1}{\phi}J\right)^{-1}
   =\phi J^{-1}
   =\phi J^{\mathsf T}
   =\phi\begin{pmatrix}0&1\\[2pt]-1&0\end{pmatrix}, \\
  \det(T^{-1})
  &=\det(T)^{-1}
   =\left(\phi^{-2}\right)^{-1}
   =\phi^{2}
\end{aligned}

  &=\chi_{n,m}(\theta,\rho), \\

  &=e^{in\theta}e^{in\pi/2}\,e^{s_{n,m}\rho}e^{-s_{n,m}\ln\phi} \\

\begin{aligned}
  e^{in\pi/2}e^{-s_{n,m}\ln\phi}=1
  &\Longleftrightarrow e^{in\pi/2-s_{n,m}\ln\phi}=1 \\
  &\Longleftrightarrow in\frac\pi2-s_{n,m}\ln\phi\in 2\pi i\,\mathbb Z \\
  &\Longleftrightarrow \exists m\in\mathbb Z:\ in\frac\pi2-s_{n,m}\ln\phi=2\pi i m \\

\begin{aligned}
  u&:=u_{0}+\eta u_{1},\qquad u_{0},u_{1}\in\mathbb C,\\
  u^{n}
  &=(u_{0}+\eta u_{1})^{n} \\
  &=\sum_{k=0}^{n}\binom{n}{k}\,u_{0}^{\,n-k}\,(\eta u_{1})^{k} \\
  &=\binom{n}{0}\,u_{0}^{\,n}\,(\eta u_{1})^{0}
   +\binom{n}{1}\,u_{0}^{\,n-1}\,(\eta u_{1})^{1}
   +\sum_{k=2}^{n}\binom{n}{k}\,u_{0}^{\,n-k}\,(\eta u_{1})^{k} \\
  &=u_{0}^{\,n}+n\,u_{0}^{\,n-1}\,\eta u_{1}
   +\sum_{k=2}^{n}\binom{n}{k}\,u_{0}^{\,n-k}\,\eta^{k}u_{1}^{k} \\
  &=u_{0}^{\,n}+n\,u_{0}^{\,n-1}\,\eta u_{1}
   +\sum_{k=2}^{n}\binom{n}{k}\,u_{0}^{\,n-k}\,(0)\,u_{1}^{k} \\
  &=u_{0}^{\,n}+n\,u_{0}^{\,n-1}\,\eta u_{1}

\[
\begin{aligned}
  e^{u}
  &:=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{u^{n}}{n!} \\
  &=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{u_{0}^{\,n}+n\,u_{0}^{\,n-1}\,\eta u_{1}}{n!} \\
  &=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{u_{0}^{\,n}}{n!}
   +\eta u_{1}\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{n\,u_{0}^{\,n-1}}{n!} \\
  &=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{u_{0}^{\,n}}{n!}
   +\eta u_{1}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{n\,u_{0}^{\,n-1}}{n!} \\
  &=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{u_{0}^{\,n}}{n!}
   +\eta u_{1}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{u_{0}^{\,n-1}}{(n-1)!} \\
  &=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{u_{0}^{\,n}}{n!}
   +\eta u_{1}\sum_{m=0}^{\infty}\frac{u_{0}^{\,m}}{m!} \\
  &=e^{u_{0}}+\eta u_{1}e^{u_{0}} \\
  &=e^{u_{0}}(1+\eta u_{1})
\end{aligned}

\[
\begin{aligned}
  u^{(d)}&:=u_{0}+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\eta_{j}u_{j},\qquad u_{0},u_{j}\in\mathbb C,\\
  (u^{(d)})^{n}
  &=\left(u_{0}+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\eta_{j}u_{j}\right)^{n} \\
  &=\sum_{k=0}^{n}\binom{n}{k}\,u_{0}^{\,n-k}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{d}\eta_{j}u_{j}\right)^{k} \\
  &=\binom{n}{0}u_{0}^{\,n}
   +\binom{n}{1}u_{0}^{\,n-1}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{d}\eta_{j}u_{j}\right)
   +\sum_{k=2}^{n}\binom{n}{k}\,u_{0}^{\,n-k}\left(\sum_{j=1}^{d}\eta_{j}u_{j}\right)^{k} \\
  &=u_{0}^{\,n}+n\,u_{0}^{\,n-1}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\eta_{j}u_{j}
   +\sum_{k=2}^{n}\binom{n}{k}\,u_{0}^{\,n-k}\,(0) \\
  &=u_{0}^{\,n}+n\,u_{0}^{\,n-1}\sum_{j=1}^{d}\eta_{j}u_{j}
\end{aligned}
\]
%%
\[
\begin{aligned}
  e^{u^{(d)}}
  &:=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{(u^{(d)})^{n}}{n!} \\
  &=\sum_{n=0}^{\infty}\frac{u_{0}^{\,n}}{n!}
   +\sum_{j=1}^{d}\eta_{j}u_{j}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{u_{0}^{\,n-1}}{(n-1)!} \\
  &=e^{u_{0}}+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\eta_{j}u_{j}e^{u_{0}} \\
  &=e^{u_{0}}\left(1+\sum_{j=1}^{d}\eta_{j}u_{j}\right)
  \tag{EXP_d}\label{EXP_d}
\end{aligned}
\]

%%
\[
\begin{aligned}
  w^{(1)}&:= (\rho+\eta\dot\rho)+i(\theta+\eta\dot\theta)
  =(\rho+i\theta)+\eta(\dot\rho+i\dot\theta)
  =w+\eta\dot w, \\
  \dot w&:=\dot\rho+i\dot\theta,\qquad w:=\rho+i\theta, \\
  z^{(1)}
  &:=e^{w^{(1)}} \\
  &=e^{w+\eta\dot w} \\
  &=e^{w}\,e^{\eta\dot w} \\
  &=e^{w}\,(1+\eta\dot w) \\
  &=z(1+\eta\dot w) \\
  &=z+\eta(z\dot w)
\end{aligned}

  0&=\Phi_{10}(\zeta)=\zeta^{4}-\zeta^{3}+\zeta^{2}-\zeta+1, \\
  0&=\zeta^{-2}\Phi_{10}(\zeta)
    =\zeta^{2}-\zeta+1-\zeta^{-1}+\zeta^{-2}, \\

  \qquad

  \qquad

  Qv_{\phi}
  &=\begin{pmatrix}1&1\\[2pt]1&0\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\phi\\[2pt]1\end{pmatrix}
   =\begin{pmatrix}\phi+1\\[2pt]\phi\end{pmatrix}
   =\begin{pmatrix}\phi^{2}\\[2pt]\phi\end{pmatrix}
   =\phi\begin{pmatrix}\phi\\[2pt]1\end{pmatrix}

  &=\begin{pmatrix}1&1\\[2pt]1&0\end{pmatrix}\begin{pmatrix}\psi\\[2pt]1\end{pmatrix}

  D=\begin{pmatrix}\phi&0\\[2pt]0&\psi\end{pmatrix}, \\

  &=\begin{pmatrix}Qv_{\phi}&Qv_{\psi}\end{pmatrix}
   =\begin{pmatrix}\phi v_{\phi}&\psi v_{\psi}\end{pmatrix}
   =\begin{pmatrix}v_{\phi}&v_{\psi}\end{pmatrix}
    \begin{pmatrix}\phi&0\\[2pt]0&\psi\end{pmatrix}
   =SD, \\
  QS=SD
  &\Longrightarrow S^{-1}QS=D
  \Longrightarrow Q=SDS^{-1}
\end{aligned}

  M_\Phi^y
  =\begin{pmatrix}
    1&0&-\frac\pi2y\\
    0&1&y\ln b\\
    0&0&1
  \end{pmatrix}, \\
  M_\Pi^xM_\Phi^y
  &=
  \begin{pmatrix}
    1\cdot 1+0\cdot 0+(-2\pi x)\cdot 0 &
    1\cdot 0+0\cdot 1+(-2\pi x)\cdot 0 &
    1\cdot(-\frac\pi2y)+0\cdot(y\ln b)+(-2\pi x)\cdot 1 \\
    0\cdot 1+1\cdot 0+(x\ln a)\cdot 0 &
    0\cdot 0+1\cdot 1+(x\ln a)\cdot 0 &
    0\cdot(-\frac\pi2y)+1\cdot(y\ln b)+(x\ln a)\cdot 1 \\
    0&0&1
  \end{pmatrix} \\
  &=
  \begin{pmatrix}
    1&0&-\left(2\pi x+\frac\pi2y\right)\\
    0&1&x\ln a+y\ln b\\
    0&0&1
  \end{pmatrix}
  =\mathsf T(\alpha,\lambda), \\
  \alpha&=2\pi x+\frac\pi2y,\qquad
  \ln\lambda=x\ln a+y\ln b,\qquad
  \lambda=e^{\ln\lambda}=e^{x\ln a+y\ln b}=e^{x\ln a}e^{y\ln b}=a^{x}b^{y}
\end{aligned}

  &=
  \frac{(\eta a)^{0}}{0!}\,f^{(0)}(w)
  +\frac{(\eta a)^{1}}{1!}\,f^{(1)}(w)
  +\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{(\eta a)^{k}}{k!}\,f^{(k)}(w) \\
  &=
  \frac{1}{1}\,f(w)
  +\frac{\eta a}{1}\,f'(w)
  +\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{\eta^{k}a^{k}}{k!}\,f^{(k)}(w) \\
  &=

  +\sum_{k=2}^{\infty}\frac{\eta^{2}\eta^{k-2}a^{k}}{k!}\,f^{(k)}(w) \\
  &=

  &=w\,\Psi'(w),\\
  \bigl((\mathsf P\mathsf Q-\mathsf Q\mathsf P)\Psi\bigr)(w)

  &=\bigl(\mathsf Q(\mathsf W(-c)\Psi)\bigr)(w+c) \\
  &=(w+c)\,(\mathsf W(-c)\Psi)(w+c) \\
  &=(w+c)\,\Psi\bigl((w+c)-c\bigr) \\
  &=(w+c)\,\Psi(w) \\

  2a\le 2^{n}x<2a+1&\Rightarrow \left\lfloor 2^{n}x\right\rfloor=2a,\\

\begin{aligned}
  \left\lfloor 2^{N}x\right\rfloor

   =2\left\lfloor 2^{N-1}x\right\rfloor+d_{N}(x)\\
  &=2\left(2\left\lfloor 2^{N-2}x\right\rfloor+d_{N-1}(x)\right)+d_{N}(x)\\
  &=2^{2}\left\lfloor 2^{N-2}x\right\rfloor+2d_{N-1}(x)+d_{N}(x)\\
  &=2^{N-k}\left\lfloor 2^{k}x\right\rfloor+\sum_{n=k+1}^{N}d_{n}(x)\,2^{N-n}
  \qquad(\forall k\in\{0,1,\dots,N-1\}),\\
  k=0:\quad

  x\in I=[0,1):\ \left\lfloor 2^{0}x\right\rfloor&=0,\\

  (f:Y\to X\text{ in }\mathcal C)
\end{aligned}
\]

\end{aligned}

  &:=\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathcal C}(-,X):\mathcal C^{\mathrm{op}}\to\mathbf{Set}

  &:=g\circ u,\qquad g\in\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathcal C}(A,X)

  \forall u_1\in\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathcal C}(A_1,A_2),\
  \forall u_2\in\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathcal C}(A_0,A_1),\
  \forall g\in\operatorname{Hom}_{\mathcal C}(A_2,X): \\
  h_X(u_2)\bigl(h_X(u_1)(g)\bigr)
  =h_X(u_2)(g\circ u_1)
  =(g\circ u_1)\circ u_2
  =g\circ(u_1\circ u_2)
  =h_X(u_1\circ u_2)(g)
\end{aligned}

  &:=f\circ g

  =f\circ(g\circ u)

  \eta_A(g)
  &=\eta_A(\mathrm{id}_X\circ g)
  =\eta_A\bigl(h_X(g)(\mathrm{id}_X)\bigr) \\
  &=\bigl(\eta_A\circ h_X(g)\bigr)(\mathrm{id}_X)
  =\bigl(h_Y(g)\circ\eta_X\bigr)(\mathrm{id}_X) \\
  &=h_Y(g)\bigl(\eta_X(\mathrm{id}_X)\bigr)

  &=v_\rho w_\rho+v_\theta w_\theta

\begin{aligned}
  \gamma:[t_0,t_1]\to M,\quad

  &=\theta'(t)\,\partial_\theta+\rho'(t)\,\partial_\rho
\end{aligned}

  &=\rho'(t)^2+\theta'(t)^2, \\

  &:=\int_{t_0}^{t_1}\sqrt{\langle \gamma'(t),\gamma'(t)\rangle_*}\,dt
  =\int_{t_0}^{t_1}\sqrt{\rho'(t)^2+\theta'(t)^2}\,dt
\end{aligned}

  w(t)&:=\rho(t)+i\theta(t), \\

  &=\rho'(t)+i\theta'(t), \\
  \left|\frac{dw}{dt}\right|^{2}
  &=(\rho'(t))^{2}+(\theta'(t))^{2}, \\
  \left|\frac{dw}{dt}\right|
  &=\sqrt{(\rho'(t))^{2}+(\theta'(t))^{2}}, \\
  L_*(\gamma)
  &=\int_{t_0}^{t_1}\left|\frac{dw}{dt}\right|\,dt

%%
\[
\begin{aligned}
  f&:=\mathbb B_{s;\alpha,\beta},\qquad s\in\{\pm1\},\qquad \alpha,\beta\in\mathbb R,\\
  f(\theta,\rho)&=(\theta',\rho')=(s\theta-\alpha,\ s\rho+\beta),\\
  d\alpha&=0,\qquad d\beta=0,\\
  d\theta'
  &=d(s\theta-\alpha)
   =s\,d\theta-d\alpha
   =s\,d\theta,\\
  d\rho'
  &=d(s\rho+\beta)
   =s\,d\rho+d\beta
   =s\,d\rho,\\
  f^*(d\rho'\otimes d\rho'+d\theta'\otimes d\theta')
  &=(f^*d\rho')\otimes(f^*d\rho')+(f^*d\theta')\otimes(f^*d\theta')\\
  &=(s\,d\rho)\otimes(s\,d\rho)+(s\,d\theta)\otimes(s\,d\theta)\\
  &=s^2(d\rho\otimes d\rho+d\theta\otimes d\theta)
   =d\rho\otimes d\rho+d\theta\otimes d\theta
   =g_*
\end{aligned}
\]
%%
\[
\begin{aligned}
  \forall \gamma:[t_0,t_1]\to M:\quad
  \gamma(t)&=(\theta(t),\rho(t)),\\
  (f\circ\gamma)(t)
  &=(s\theta(t)-\alpha,\ s\rho(t)+\beta),\\
  \frac{d}{dt}(s\theta(t)-\alpha)&=s\theta'(t),\qquad
  \frac{d}{dt}(s\rho(t)+\beta)=s\rho'(t),\\
  L_*(f\circ\gamma)
  &=\int_{t_0}^{t_1}\sqrt{(s\rho'(t))^2+(s\theta'(t))^2}\,dt\\
  &=\int_{t_0}^{t_1}\sqrt{s^2\bigl((\rho'(t))^2+(\theta'(t))^2\bigr)}\,dt\\
  &=\int_{t_0}^{t_1}\sqrt{(\rho'(t))^2+(\theta'(t))^2}\,dt\\
  &=L_*(\gamma)
\end{aligned}
\]

\[
  b:=\phi,\qquad a:=2\pi,\qquad \DelSigma:=ab,\qquad \sigma:=\DelSigma,\qquad \alpha:=\DelSigma^{-1}b=a^{-1}
\]
\[
  A^{k+1}=A^k\oplus A^k
\]
\[
  M_{\to}^{(A,k)}:A^k\to A^{k+1},\qquad
  M_{\to}^{(A,k)}(x):=\binom{bx}{\alpha x}
\]
\[
  L_{k+1}:A^{k+1}\to A^k,\qquad
  L_{k+1}\binom{u}{v}:=u-\sigma v
\]
\[
\begin{aligned}
  (L_{k+1}M_{\to}^{(A,k)})(x)
  &=L_{k+1}\binom{bx}{\alpha x}
  =bx-\sigma\alpha x
  =bx-(ab)a^{-1}x
  =bx-bx=0
\end{aligned}
\]
\[
  \mathfrak D_{k+1}:=\ker L_{k+1}
  =\left\{\binom{u}{v}\in A^{k+1}:u=\sigma v\right\}
\]
%%
\[
  M_{\leftarrow,1}^{(A,k)}:A^{k+1}\to A^k,\qquad
  M_{\leftarrow,1}^{(A,k)}\binom{u}{v}:=b^{-1}u
\]
\[
  M_{\leftarrow,2}^{(A,k)}:A^{k+1}\to A^k,\qquad
  M_{\leftarrow,2}^{(A,k)}\binom{u}{v}:=\alpha^{-1}v=av
\]
\[
\begin{aligned}
  \binom{u}{v}\in\mathfrak D_{k+1}
  &\Longrightarrow u=\sigma v
  \Longrightarrow b^{-1}u=b^{-1}\sigma v=b^{-1}ab\,v=av=\alpha^{-1}v
\end{aligned}
\]
\[
  M_{\leftarrow,1}^{(A,k)}\big|_{\mathfrak D_{k+1}}
  =M_{\leftarrow,2}^{(A,k)}\big|_{\mathfrak D_{k+1}}
\]
%%
\[
\begin{aligned}
  (M_{\leftarrow,1}^{(A,k)}M_{\to}^{(A,k)})(x)
  &=M_{\leftarrow,1}^{(A,k)}\binom{bx}{a^{-1}x}
  =b^{-1}bx=x\\
  (M_{\leftarrow,2}^{(A,k)}M_{\to}^{(A,k)})(x)
  &=M_{\leftarrow,2}^{(A,k)}\binom{bx}{a^{-1}x}
  =aa^{-1}x=x
\end{aligned}
\]
\[
\begin{aligned}
  \binom{u}{v}\in\mathfrak D_{k+1}
  &\Longrightarrow
  (M_{\to}^{(A,k)}M_{\leftarrow,1}^{(A,k)})\binom{u}{v}
  =M_{\to}^{(A,k)}(b^{-1}u)
  =\binom{u}{a^{-1}b^{-1}u}
  =\binom{u}{a^{-1}b^{-1}ab\,v}
  =\binom{u}{v}
\end{aligned}
\]
\[
\begin{aligned}
  \binom{u}{v}\in\mathfrak D_{k+1}
  &\Longrightarrow
  (M_{\to}^{(A,k)}M_{\leftarrow,2}^{(A,k)})\binom{u}{v}
  =M_{\to}^{(A,k)}(av)
  =\binom{bav}{a^{-1}av}
  =\binom{\sigma v}{v}
  =\binom{u}{v}
\end{aligned}

\kappa_1+\cdots+\kappa_q=m\\
1\kappa_1+2\kappa_2+\cdots+q\kappa_q=q}}
\frac{m!}{\kappa_1!\cdots \kappa_q!}
\prod_{j=1}^{q}\left(\frac{f^{(j)}(w)}{j!}\right)^{\kappa_j},

&=\exp\!\Bigl(-i\,\frac{2\pi m}{\ln\varphi}\,(\rho+t\ln\varphi)\Bigr)\;
\Psi\!\bigl(U_\varphi^t(\theta,\rho)\bigr)\\
&=\exp\!\Bigl(-i\,\frac{2\pi m}{\ln\varphi}\,\rho\Bigr)\;
\exp\!\Bigl(-i\,\frac{2\pi m}{\ln\varphi}\,t\ln\varphi\Bigr)\;

&=\exp\!\Bigl(-i\,\frac{2\pi m}{\ln\varphi}\,\rho\Bigr)\;
\exp\!\bigl(-i\,2\pi m t\bigr)\;
\Psi\!\bigl(U_\varphi^t(\theta,\rho)\bigr)\\
&=\exp\!\bigl(-i\,2\pi m t\bigr)\;
\exp\!\Bigl(-i\,\frac{2\pi m}{\ln\varphi}\,\rho\Bigr)\;
(U_t\Psi)(\theta,\rho)\\
&=\exp\!\bigl(-i\,2\pi m t\bigr)\;
(M_m(U_t\Psi))(\theta,\rho)\\
&=\bigl((e^{-i2\pi m t}M_mU_t)\Psi\bigr)(\theta,\rho)
